Filter data in preheat zone (100°C to 150°C)\n            preheat_data = tc_data[(tc_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n                                    (tc_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)].copy()\n\n            if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n            preheat_data = preheat_data.sort_values('time_s')\n            time_diff = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n            temp_diff = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n\n            # Calculate ramp rate in °C/s\n            ramp_rates = temp_diff / time_diff\n\n            # Find maximum ramp rate for this TC\n            if len(ramp_rates.dropna()) > 0:\n                max_ramp = ramp_rates.max()\n\n                if max_ramp > max_ramp_overall:\n                    max_ramp_overall = max_ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Store results for this run\n        if max_ramp_tc is not None:\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_overall, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violating\n            if max_ramp_overall > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n                results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n                print(f\"  {run_id}: {max_ramp_overall:.2f} °C/s (TC: {max_ramp_tc}) - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            # No preheat data found\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": None\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No preheat data found\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal violations: {len(results['violating_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q02_tal(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL = time spent above liquidus temperature\n    Required range: 30-60 seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get liquidus temperature for each run from MES log\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No MES data found\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # For TAL, we typically use the thermocouple at the largest mass location\n        # as it represents the most conservative measurement\n        # According to handbook: use representative thermocouple\n\n        # Prefer TC at largest_mass location, otherwise use first available\n        tc_largest = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].unique()\n        if len(tc_largest) > 0:\n            selected_tc = tc_largest[0]\n        else:\n            selected_tc = tc_ids[0]\n\n        tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == selected_tc].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time periods above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # Calculate total time above liquidus\n            # Assuming time points are evenly spaced, sum up the intervals\n            time_sorted = above_liquidus['time_s'].sort_values()\n\n            # Find continuous segments\n            if len(time_sorted) == 1:\n                tal_s = 0.0  # Single point, no duration\n            else:\n                # For TAL, we need to find the main reflow event\n                # typically this is the longest continuous segment above liquidus\n                time_values = time_sorted.values\n                time_diffs = time_values[1:] - time_values[:-1]\n\n                # Typical sampling interval\n                median_interval = 10.0  # seconds (from data inspection)\n\n                # Find continuous segments (gaps < 2x median interval)\n                segments = []\n                current_segment_start = time_values[0]\n                current_segment_end = time_values[0]\n\n                for i in range(len(time_diffs)):\n                    if time_diffs[i] <= 2 * median_interval:\n                        # Continue current segment\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n                    else:\n                        # End current segment, start new one\n                        segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n                        current_segment_start = time_values[i + 1]\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n\n                # Add final segment\n                segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n\n                # TAL is the longest segment duration\n                tal_s = max([end - start for start, end in segments])\n\n        status = \"compliant\" if TAL_MIN <= tal_s <= TAL_MAX else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": selected_tc,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": TAL_MIN,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": TAL_MAX,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status_str = \"COMPLIANT\" if status == \"compliant\" else \"NON-COMPLIANT\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: TAL = {tal_s:.2f}s (TC: {selected_tc}) - {status_str}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"\\nCompliant: {compliant_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q03_peak_temp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    Peak temp requirement: liquidus + 20°C\n    If no thermocouple data, run is failing.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Compliance\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + MIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAILING\")\n            continue\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs (most conservative)\n        min_peak = 999999\n        min_peak_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n            peak_temp = tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n            if peak_temp < min_peak:\n                min_peak = peak_temp\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets_requirement = min_peak >= required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if not meets_requirement:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAILING\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASSING\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailing: {len(results['failing_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    From handbook:\n    - LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n    - PROCESS SPEED must be >= LINE SPEED (minimum)\n\n    The actual conveyor speed is given in the MES log.\n    We need to check if it meets the minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get parameters\n        boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = mes_row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = mes_row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = mes_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        meets_str = \"MEETS\" if meets else \"FAILS\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: Actual = {actual_speed:.2f} cm/min, Required >= {required_min_speed:.2f} cm/min - {meets_str}\")\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"\\nMeets requirement: {meets_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q05_best_run_per_family(thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects, q01_results, q02_results, q03_results, q04_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Quality metrics:\n    - Profile compliance (preheat ramp < 2°C/s)\n    - TAL in range (30-60s)\n    - Peak temp meets requirement\n    - Defect yield (from test_defects)\n\n    Efficiency metrics:\n    - Conveyor speed feasibility\n    - Throughput (boards_per_min)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection Per Board Family\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all board families\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        print(f\"\\n  Board Family: {board_family}\")\n\n        # Get all runs for this family\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n        if len(family_runs) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = {}\n\n        for run_id in family_runs:\n            score = 0\n            violations = []\n\n            # Quality metrics\n\n            # 1. Preheat ramp compliance\n            if run_id not in q01_results['violating_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"preheat_ramp\")\n\n            # 2. TAL compliance\n            tal_result = next((r for r in q02_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if tal_result and tal_result['status'] == 'compliant':\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"tal\")\n\n            # 3. Peak temp compliance\n            if run_id not in q03_results['failing_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"peak_temp\")\n\n            # 4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n            speed_result = next((r for r in q04_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if speed_result and speed_result['meets']:\n                score += 5\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"conveyor_speed\")\n\n            # 5. Defect yield (from test_defects)\n            defect_summary = test_defects[(test_defects['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                          (test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n            if len(defect_summary) > 0:\n                yield_pct = defect_summary['fp_yield_pct'].values[0]\n                # Higher yield is better\n                score += yield_pct / 10  # Scale to 0-10 range\n\n            # Efficiency metrics\n\n            # 6. Throughput (boards per minute)\n            mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n            score += boards_per_min  # Higher throughput is better\n\n            # 7. Downtime penalty\n            downtime_s = mes_row['downtime_s']\n            score -= downtime_s / 10  # Penalize downtime\n\n            run_scores[run_id] = score\n\n            print(f\"    {run_id}: Score = {score:.2f}, Violations = {violations}\")\n\n        # Select best run (highest score)\n        if len(run_scores) > 0:\n            sorted_runs = sorted(run_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            best_run_id = sorted_runs[0][0]\n            runner_up_ids = [run_id for run_id, _ in sorted_runs[1:]]\n\n            result = {\n                \"board_family\": board_family,\n                \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n                \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_ids\n            }\n\n            results.append(result)\n\n            print(f\"    Best run: {best_run_id} (Score: {run_scores[best_run_id]:.2f})\")\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE MAINTENANCE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    # Question 2: Time above liquidus\n    q02_results = calculate_q02_tal(thermocouples, mes_log)\n    with open(output_dir / 'q02.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q02_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Q02 results saved to {output_dir / 'q02.json'}\")\n\n    # Question 4: Conveyor speed\n    q04_results = calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log)\n    with open(output_dir / 'q04.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(q04_results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\n✓ Q04 results saved to {output_dir / 'q04.json'}\")\n\n    # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET = 20.0  # °C above liquidus\nTAL_MIN = 30.0  # seconds\nTAL_MAX = 60.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all necessary data files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    return thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects\n\n\ndef calculate_q01_preheat_ramp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone: 100°C to 150°C\n    Maximum allowable: 2°C/s\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        max_ramp_overall = -999999\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id].sort_values('time_s')\n\n            # Filter data in preheat zone (100°C to 150°C)\n            preheat_data = tc_data[(tc_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n                                    (tc_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)].copy()\n\n            if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n            preheat_data = preheat_data.sort_values('time_s')\n            time_diff = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n            temp_diff = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n\n            # Calculate ramp rate in °C/s\n            ramp_rates = temp_diff / time_diff\n\n            # Find maximum ramp rate for this TC\n            if len(ramp_rates.dropna()) > 0:\n                max_ramp = ramp_rates.max()\n\n                if max_ramp > max_ramp_overall:\n                    max_ramp_overall = max_ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Store results for this run\n        if max_ramp_tc is not None:\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_overall, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violating\n            if max_ramp_overall > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n                results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n                print(f\"  {run_id}: {max_ramp_overall:.2f} °C/s (TC: {max_ramp_tc}) - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            # No preheat data found\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": None\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No preheat data found\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal violations: {len(results['violating_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q02_tal(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL = time spent above liquidus temperature\n    Required range: 30-60 seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get liquidus temperature for each run from MES log\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No MES data found\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # For TAL, we typically use the thermocouple at the largest mass location\n        # as it represents the most conservative measurement\n        # According to handbook: use representative thermocouple\n\n        # Prefer TC at largest_mass location, otherwise use first available\n        tc_largest = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].unique()\n        if len(tc_largest) > 0:\n            selected_tc = tc_largest[0]\n        else:\n            selected_tc = tc_ids[0]\n\n        tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == selected_tc].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time periods above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # Calculate total time above liquidus\n            # Assuming time points are evenly spaced, sum up the intervals\n            time_sorted = above_liquidus['time_s'].sort_values()\n\n            # Find continuous segments\n            if len(time_sorted) == 1:\n                tal_s = 0.0  # Single point, no duration\n            else:\n                # For TAL, we need to find the main reflow event\n                # typically this is the longest continuous segment above liquidus\n                time_values = time_sorted.values\n                time_diffs = time_values[1:] - time_values[:-1]\n\n                # Typical sampling interval\n                median_interval = 10.0  # seconds (from data inspection)\n\n                # Find continuous segments (gaps < 2x median interval)\n                segments = []\n                current_segment_start = time_values[0]\n                current_segment_end = time_values[0]\n\n                for i in range(len(time_diffs)):\n                    if time_diffs[i] <= 2 * median_interval:\n                        # Continue current segment\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n                    else:\n                        # End current segment, start new one\n                        segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n                        current_segment_start = time_values[i + 1]\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n\n                # Add final segment\n                segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n\n                # TAL is the longest segment duration\n                tal_s = max([end - start for start, end in segments])\n\n        status = \"compliant\" if TAL_MIN <= tal_s <= TAL_MAX else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": selected_tc,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": TAL_MIN,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": TAL_MAX,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status_str = \"COMPLIANT\" if status == \"compliant\" else \"NON-COMPLIANT\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: TAL = {tal_s:.2f}s (TC: {selected_tc}) - {status_str}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"\\nCompliant: {compliant_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q03_peak_temp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    Peak temp requirement: liquidus + 20°C\n    If no thermocouple data, run is failing.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Compliance\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + MIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAILING\")\n            continue\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs (most conservative)\n        min_peak = 999999\n        min_peak_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n            peak_temp = tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n            if peak_temp < min_peak:\n                min_peak = peak_temp\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets_requirement = min_peak >= required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if not meets_requirement:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAILING\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASSING\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailing: {len(results['failing_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    From handbook:\n    - LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n    - PROCESS SPEED must be >= LINE SPEED (minimum)\n\n    The actual conveyor speed is given in the MES log.\n    We need to check if it meets the minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get parameters\n        boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = mes_row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = mes_row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = mes_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        meets_str = \"MEETS\" if meets else \"FAILS\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: Actual = {actual_speed:.2f} cm/min, Required >= {required_min_speed:.2f} cm/min - {meets_str}\")\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"\\nMeets requirement: {meets_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q05_best_run_per_family(thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects, q01_results, q02_results, q03_results, q04_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Quality metrics:\n    - Profile compliance (preheat ramp < 2°C/s)\n    - TAL in range (30-60s)\n    - Peak temp meets requirement\n    - Defect yield (from test_defects)\n\n    Efficiency metrics:\n    - Conveyor speed feasibility\n    - Throughput (boards_per_min)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection Per Board Family\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all board families\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        print(f\"\\n  Board Family: {board_family}\")\n\n        # Get all runs for this family\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n        if len(family_runs) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = {}\n\n        for run_id in family_runs:\n            score = 0\n            violations = []\n\n            # Quality metrics\n\n            # 1. Preheat ramp compliance\n            if run_id not in q01_results['violating_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"preheat_ramp\")\n\n            # 2. TAL compliance\n            tal_result = next((r for r in q02_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if tal_result and tal_result['status'] == 'compliant':\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"tal\")\n\n            # 3. Peak temp compliance\n            if run_id not in q03_results['failing_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"peak_temp\")\n\n            # 4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n            speed_result = next((r for r in q04_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if speed_result and speed_result['meets']:\n                score += 5\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"conveyor_speed\")\n\n            # 5. Defect yield (from test_defects)\n            defect_summary = test_defects[(test_defects['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                          (test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n            if len(defect_summary) > 0:\n                yield_pct = defect_summary['fp_yield_pct'].values[0]\n                # Higher yield is better\n                score += yield_pct / 10  # Scale to 0-10 range\n\n            # Efficiency metrics\n\n            # 6. Throughput (boards per minute)\n            mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n            score += boards_per_min  # Higher throughput is better\n\n            # 7. Downtime penalty\n            downtime_s = mes_row['downtime_s']\n            score -= downtime_s / 10  # Penalize downtime\n\n            run_scores[run_id] = score\n\n            print(f\"    {run_id}: Score = {score:.2f}, Violations = {violations}\")\n\n        # Select best run (highest score)\n        if len(run_scores) > 0:\n            sorted_runs = sorted(run_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            best_run_id = sorted_runs[0][0]\n            runner_up_ids = [run_id for run_id, _ in sorted_runs[1:]]\n\n            result = {\n                \"board_family\": board_family,\n                \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n                \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_ids\n            }\n\n            results.append(result)\n\n            print(f\"    Best run: {best_run_id} (Score: {run_scores[best_run_id]:.2f})\")\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE MAINTENANCE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET = 20.0  # °C above liquidus\nTAL_MIN = 30.0  # seconds\nTAL_MAX = 60.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all necessary data files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    return thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects\n\n\ndef calculate_q01_preheat_ramp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone: 100°C to 150°C\n    Maximum allowable: 2°C/s\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Calculate ramp rate in °C/s\n            ramp_rates = temp_diff / time_diff\n\n            # Find maximum ramp rate for this TC\n            if len(ramp_rates.dropna()) > 0:\n                max_ramp = ramp_rates.max()\n\n                if max_ramp > max_ramp_overall:\n                    max_ramp_overall = max_ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        # No preheat data found\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": None\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No preheat data found\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal violations: {len(results['violating_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q02_tal(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL = time spent above liquidus temperature\n    Required range: 30-60 seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get liquidus temperature for each run from MES log\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No MES data found\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # For TAL, we typically use the thermocouple at the largest mass location\n        # as it represents the most conservative measurement\n        # According to handbook: use representative thermocouple\n\n        # Prefer TC at largest_mass location, otherwise use first available\n        tc_largest = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].unique()\n        if len(tc_largest) > 0:\n            selected_tc = tc_largest[0]\n        else:\n            selected_tc = tc_ids[0]\n\n        tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == selected_tc].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time periods above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # Calculate total time above liquidus\n            # Assuming time points are evenly spaced, sum up the intervals\n            time_sorted = above_liquidus['time_s'].sort_values()\n\n            # Find continuous segments\n            if len(time_sorted) == 1:\n                tal_s = 0.0  # Single point, no duration\n            else:\n                # For TAL, we need to find the main reflow event\n                # typically this is the longest continuous segment above liquidus\n                time_values = time_sorted.values\n                time_diffs = time_values[1:] - time_values[:-1]\n\n                # Typical sampling interval\n                median_interval = 10.0  # seconds (from data inspection)\n\n                # Find continuous segments (gaps < 2x median interval)\n                segments = []\n                current_segment_start = time_values[0]\n                current_segment_end = time_values[0]\n\n                for i in range(len(time_diffs)):\n                    if time_diffs[i] <= 2 * median_interval:\n                        # Continue current segment\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n                    else:\n                        # End current segment, start new one\n                        segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n                        current_segment_start = time_values[i + 1]\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n\n                # Add final segment\n                segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n\n                # TAL is the longest segment duration\n                tal_s = max([end - start for start, end in segments])\n\n        status = \"compliant\" if TAL_MIN <= tal_s <= TAL_MAX else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": selected_tc,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": TAL_MIN,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": TAL_MAX,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status_str = \"COMPLIANT\" if status == \"compliant\" else \"NON-COMPLIANT\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: TAL = {tal_s:.2f}s (TC: {selected_tc}) - {status_str}\")\n\n    # No thermocouple data - failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAILING\")\n            continue\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs (most conservative)\n        min_peak = 999999\n        min_peak_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n            peak_temp = tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n            if peak_temp < min_peak:\n                min_peak = peak_temp\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets_requirement = min_peak >= required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if not meets_requirement:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAILING\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASSING\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailing: {len(results['failing_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    From handbook:\n    - LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n    - PROCESS SPEED must be >= LINE SPEED (minimum)\n\n    The actual conveyor speed is given in the MES log.\n    We need to check if it meets the minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        meets_str = \"MEETS\" if meets else \"FAILS\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: Actual = {actual_speed:.2f} cm/min, Required >= {required_min_speed:.2f} cm/min - {meets_str}\")\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"\\nMeets requirement: {meets_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q05_best_run_per_family(thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects, q01_results, q02_results, q03_results, q04_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Quality metrics:\n    - Profile compliance (preheat ramp < 2°C/s)\n    - TAL in range (30-60s)\n    - Peak temp meets requirement\n    - Defect yield (from test_defects)\n\n    Efficiency metrics:\n    - Conveyor speed feasibility\n    - Throughput (boards_per_min)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection Per Board Family\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Score each run\n        run_scores = {}\n\n        for run_id in family_runs:\n            score = 0\n            violations = []\n\n            # Quality metrics\n\n            # 1. Preheat ramp compliance\n            if run_id not in q01_results['violating_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"preheat_ramp\")\n\n            # 2. TAL compliance\n            tal_result = next((r for r in q02_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if tal_result and tal_result['status'] == 'compliant':\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"tal\")\n\n            # 3. Peak temp compliance\n            if run_id not in q03_results['failing_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"peak_temp\")\n\n            # 4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n            speed_result = next((r for r in q04_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if speed_result and speed_result['meets']:\n                score += 5\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"conveyor_speed\")\n\n            # 5. Defect yield (from test_defects)\n            defect_summary = test_defects[(test_defects['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                          (test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n            if len(defect_summary) > 0:\n                yield_pct = defect_summary['fp_yield_pct'].values[0]\n                # Higher yield is better\n                score += yield_pct / 10  # Scale to 0-10 range\n\n            # Efficiency metrics\n\n            # 6. Throughput (boards per minute)\n            mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n            score += boards_per_min  # Higher throughput is better\n\n            # 7. Downtime penalty\n            downtime_s = mes_row['downtime_s']\n            score -= downtime_s / 10  # Penalize downtime\n\n            run_scores[run_id] = score\n\n            print(f\"    {run_id}: Score = {score:.2f}, Violations = {violations}\")\n\n        # Select best run (highest score)\n        if len(run_scores) > 0:\n            sorted_runs = sorted(run_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            best_run_id = sorted_runs[0][0]\n            runner_up_ids = [run_id for run_id, _ in sorted_runs[1:]]\n\n            result = {\n                \"board_family\": board_family,\n                \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n                \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_ids\n            }\n\n            results.append(result)\n\n            print(f\"    Best run: {best_run_id} (Score: {run_scores[best_run_id]:.2f})\")\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE MAINTENANCE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    # °C\nPREHEAT_TEMP_MAX = 150.0  # °C\nMAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE = 2.0  # °C/s\nMIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET = 20.0  # °C above liquidus\nTAL_MIN = 30.0  # seconds\nTAL_MAX = 60.0  # seconds\n\ndef load_data():\n    \"\"\"Load all necessary data files.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading data files...\")\n    thermocouples = pd.read_csv('/app/data/thermocouples.csv')\n    mes_log = pd.read_csv('/app/data/mes_log.csv')\n    test_defects = pd.read_csv('/app/data/test_defects.csv')\n\n    return thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects\n\n\ndef calculate_q01_preheat_ramp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 1: Calculate maximum temperature ramp rate during preheat for each run.\n\n    Preheat zone: 100°C to 150°C\n    Maximum allowable: 2°C/s\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 1: Maximum Preheat Ramp Rate Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"ramp_rate_limit_c_per_s\": MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE,\n        \"violating_runs\": [],\n        \"max_ramp_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        max_ramp_overall = -999999\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id].sort_values('time_s')\n\n            # Filter data in preheat zone (100°C to 150°C)\n            preheat_data = tc_data[(tc_data['temp_c'] >= PREHEAT_TEMP_MIN) &\n                                    (tc_data['temp_c'] <= PREHEAT_TEMP_MAX)].copy()\n\n            if len(preheat_data) < 2:\n                continue\n\n            # Calculate ramp rates between consecutive points\n            preheat_data = preheat_data.sort_values('time_s')\n            time_diff = preheat_data['time_s'].diff()\n            temp_diff = preheat_data['temp_c'].diff()\n\n            # Calculate ramp rate in °C/s\n            ramp_rates = temp_diff / time_diff\n\n            # Find maximum ramp rate for this TC\n            if len(ramp_rates.dropna()) > 0:\n                max_ramp = ramp_rates.max()\n\n                if max_ramp > max_ramp_overall:\n                    max_ramp_overall = max_ramp\n                    max_ramp_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Store results for this run\n        if max_ramp_tc is not None:\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": max_ramp_tc,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_overall, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violating\n            if max_ramp_overall > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n                results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n                print(f\"  {run_id}: {max_ramp_overall:.2f} °C/s (TC: {max_ramp_tc}) - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            # No preheat data found\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": None\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No preheat data found\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nTotal violations: {len(results['violating_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q02_tal(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL = time spent above liquidus temperature\n    Required range: 30-60 seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get liquidus temperature for each run from MES log\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No MES data found\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # For TAL, we typically use the thermocouple at the largest mass location\n        # as it represents the most conservative measurement\n        # According to handbook: use representative thermocouple\n\n        # Prefer TC at largest_mass location, otherwise use first available\n        tc_largest = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].unique()\n        if len(tc_largest) > 0:\n            selected_tc = tc_largest[0]\n        else:\n            selected_tc = tc_ids[0]\n\n        tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == selected_tc].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time periods above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # Calculate total time above liquidus\n            # Assuming time points are evenly spaced, sum up the intervals\n            time_sorted = above_liquidus['time_s'].sort_values()\n\n            # Find continuous segments\n            if len(time_sorted) == 1:\n                tal_s = 0.0  # Single point, no duration\n            else:\n                # For TAL, we need to find the main reflow event\n                # typically this is the longest continuous segment above liquidus\n                time_values = time_sorted.values\n                time_diffs = time_values[1:] - time_values[:-1]\n\n                # Typical sampling interval\n                median_interval = 10.0  # seconds (from data inspection)\n\n                # Find continuous segments (gaps < 2x median interval)\n                segments = []\n                current_segment_start = time_values[0]\n                current_segment_end = time_values[0]\n\n                for i in range(len(time_diffs)):\n                    if time_diffs[i] <= 2 * median_interval:\n                        # Continue current segment\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n                    else:\n                        # End current segment, start new one\n                        segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n                        current_segment_start = time_values[i + 1]\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n\n                # Add final segment\n                segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n\n                # TAL is the longest segment duration\n                tal_s = max([end - start for start, end in segments])\n\n        status = \"compliant\" if TAL_MIN <= tal_s <= TAL_MAX else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": selected_tc,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": TAL_MIN,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": TAL_MAX,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status_str = \"COMPLIANT\" if status == \"compliant\" else \"NON-COMPLIANT\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: TAL = {tal_s:.2f}s (TC: {selected_tc}) - {status_str}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['status'] == 'compliant')\n    print(f\"\\nCompliant: {compliant_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q03_peak_temp(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    Peak temp requirement: liquidus + 20°C\n    If no thermocouple data, run is failing.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Compliance\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n        required_min_peak = liquidus_temp + MIN_PEAK_TEMP_OFFSET\n\n        if len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAILING\")\n            continue\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs (most conservative)\n        min_peak = 999999\n        min_peak_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n            peak_temp = tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n            if peak_temp < min_peak:\n                min_peak = peak_temp\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets_requirement = min_peak >= required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if not meets_requirement:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAILING\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASSING\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailing: {len(results['failing_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    From handbook:\n    - LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n    - PROCESS SPEED must be >= LINE SPEED (minimum)\n\n    The actual conveyor speed is given in the MES log.\n    We need to check if it meets the minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get parameters\n        boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = mes_row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = mes_row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = mes_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        meets_str = \"MEETS\" if meets else \"FAILS\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: Actual = {actual_speed:.2f} cm/min, Required >= {required_min_speed:.2f} cm/min - {meets_str}\")\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"\\nMeets requirement: {meets_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q05_best_run_per_family(thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects, q01_results, q02_results, q03_results, q04_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Quality metrics:\n    - Profile compliance (preheat ramp < 2°C/s)\n    - TAL in range (30-60s)\n    - Peak temp meets requirement\n    - Defect yield (from test_defects)\n\n    Efficiency metrics:\n    - Conveyor speed feasibility\n    - Throughput (boards_per_min)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection Per Board Family\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all board families\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        print(f\"\\n  Board Family: {board_family}\")\n\n        # Get all runs for this family\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n        if len(family_runs) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = {}\n\n        for run_id in family_runs:\n            score = 0\n            violations = []\n\n            # Quality metrics\n\n            # 1. Preheat ramp compliance\n            if run_id not in q01_results['violating_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"preheat_ramp\")\n\n            # 2. TAL compliance\n            tal_result = next((r for r in q02_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if tal_result and tal_result['status'] == 'compliant':\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"tal\")\n\n            # 3. Peak temp compliance\n            if run_id not in q03_results['failing_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"peak_temp\")\n\n            # 4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n            speed_result = next((r for r in q04_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if speed_result and speed_result['meets']:\n                score += 5\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"conveyor_speed\")\n\n            # 5. Defect yield (from test_defects)\n            defect_summary = test_defects[(test_defects['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                          (test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n            if len(defect_summary) > 0:\n                yield_pct = defect_summary['fp_yield_pct'].values[0]\n                # Higher yield is better\n                score += yield_pct / 10  # Scale to 0-10 range\n\n            # Efficiency metrics\n\n            # 6. Throughput (boards per minute)\n            mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n            score += boards_per_min  # Higher throughput is better\n\n            # 7. Downtime penalty\n            downtime_s = mes_row['downtime_s']\n            score -= downtime_s / 10  # Penalize downtime\n\n            run_scores[run_id] = score\n\n            print(f\"    {run_id}: Score = {score:.2f}, Violations = {violations}\")\n\n        # Select best run (highest score)\n        if len(run_scores) > 0:\n            sorted_runs = sorted(run_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            best_run_id = sorted_runs[0][0]\n            runner_up_ids = [run_id for run_id, _ in sorted_runs[1:]]\n\n            result = {\n                \"board_family\": board_family,\n                \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n                \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_ids\n            }\n\n            results.append(result)\n\n            print(f\"    Best run: {best_run_id} (Score: {run_scores[best_run_id]:.2f})\")\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE MAINTENANCE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    #  }\n\n    # Get all unique runs\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        max_ramp_overall = -999999\n        max_ramp_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id].sort_values('time_s')\n\n            # Filter data in preheat zone (100°C to 150°C)\n            preheat_data = tc_data[(tc_data['temp_c'] >= len(ramp_rates.dropna()) > 0:\n                max_ramp = ramp_rates.max()\n\n                if max_ramp > max_ramp_overall:\n                    max_ramp_overall = max_ramp\n         \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": round(max_ramp_overall, 2)\n            }\n\n            # Check if violating\n            if max_ramp_overall > MAX_PREHEAT_RAMP_RATE:\n                results[\"violating_runs\"].append(run_id)\n                print(f\"  {run_id}: {max_ramp_overall:.2f} °C/s (TC: {max_ramp_tc}) - VIOLATION\")\n        else:\n            # No preheat data found\n            results[\"max_ramp_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"max_preheat_ramp_c_per_s\": None\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No preheat data Question 2: Calculate time above liquidus (TAL) for each run.\n\n    TAL = time spent above liquidus temperature\n    Required range: 30-60 seconds\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 2: Time Above Liquidus (TAL) Analysis\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get liquidus temperature for each run from MES log\n    all_runs = sorted(thermocouples['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        run_data = thermocouples[thermocouples['run_id'] == run_id].copy()\n\n        # Get liquidus temperature from MES log\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id]\n        if len(mes_row) == 0:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No MES data found\")\n            continue\n\n        liquidus_temp = mes_row['solder_liquidus_c'].values[0]\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs for this run\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # For TAL, we typically use the thermocouple at the largest mass location\n        # as it represents the most conservative measurement\n        # According to handbook: use representative thermocouple\n\n        # Prefer TC at largest_mass location, otherwise use first available\n        tc_largest = run_data[run_data['tc_location'] == 'largest_mass']['tc_id'].unique()\n        if len(tc_largest) > 0:\n            selected_tc = tc_largest[0]\n        else:\n            selected_tc = tc_ids[0]\n\n        tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == selected_tc].sort_values('time_s')\n\n        # Find time periods above liquidus\n        above_liquidus = tc_data[tc_data['temp_c'] > liquidus_temp].copy()\n\n        if len(above_liquidus) == 0:\n            tal_s = 0.0\n        else:\n            # Calculate total time above liquidus\n            # Assuming time points are evenly spaced, sum up the intervals\n            time_sorted = above_liquidus['time_s'].sort_values()\n\n            # Find continuous segments\n            if len(time_sorted) == 1:\n                tal_s = 0.0  # Single point, no duration\n            else:\n                # For TAL, we need to find the main reflow event\n                # typically this is the longest continuous segment above liquidus\n                time_values = time_sorted.values\n                time_diffs = time_values[1:] - time_values[:-1]\n\n                # Typical sampling interval\n                median_interval = 10.0  # seconds (from data inspection)\n\n                # Find continuous segments (gaps < 2x median interval)\n                segments = []\n                current_segment_start = time_values[0]\n                current_segment_end = time_values[0]\n\n                for i in range(len(time_diffs)):\n                    if time_diffs[i] <= 2 * median_interval:\n                        # Continue current segment\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n                    else:\n                        # End current segment, start new one\n                        segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n                        current_segment_start = time_values[i + 1]\n                        current_segment_end = time_values[i + 1]\n\n                # Add final segment\n                segments.append((current_segment_start, current_segment_end))\n\n                # TAL is the longest segment duration\n                tal_s = max([end - start for start, end in segments])\n\n        status = \"compliant\" if TAL_MIN <= tal_s <= TAL_MAX else \"non-compliant\"\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"tc_id\": selected_tc,\n            \"tal_s\": round(tal_s, 2),\n            \"required_min_tal_s\": TAL_MIN,\n            \"required_max_tal_s\": TAL_MAX,\n            \"status\": status\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        status_str = \"COMPLIANT\" if status == \"compliant\" else \"NON-COMPLIANT\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: TAL = {tal_s:.2f}s (TC: {selected_tc}) - {status_str}\")\n\n    # Sort by run_id\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['run_id'])\n\n    compliant_count = sum(1 for r in results    Question 3: Check whether each run meets peak temperature requirement.\n\n    Peak temp requirement: liquidus + 20°C\n    If no thermocouple data, run is failing.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 3: Peak Temperature Compliance\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = {\n        \"failing_runs\": [],\n        \"min_peak_by_run\": {}\n    }\n\n    all_runs = len(run_data) == 0:\n            # No thermocouple data - failing\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n                \"tc_id\": None,\n                \"peak_temp_c\": None,\n                \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n            }\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: No thermocouple data - FAILING\")\n            continue\n\n        # Get unique thermocouple IDs\n        tc_ids = run_data['tc_id'].unique()\n\n        # Find minimum peak across all TCs (most conservative)\n        min_peak = 999999\n        min_peak_tc = None\n\n        for tc_id in tc_ids:\n            tc_data = run_data[run_data['tc_id'] == tc_id]\n            peak_temp = tc_data['temp_c'].max()\n\n            if peak_temp < min_peak:\n                min_peak = peak_temp\n                min_peak_tc = tc_id\n\n        # Check if meets requirement\n        meets_requirement = min_peak >= required_min_peak\n\n        results[\"min_peak_by_run\"][run_id] = {\n            \"tc_id\": min_peak_tc,\n            \"peak_temp_c\": round(min_peak, 2),\n            \"required_min_peak_c\": round(required_min_peak, 2)\n        }\n\n        if not meets_requirement:\n            results[\"failing_runs\"].append(run_id)\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - FAILING\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  {run_id}: Peak = {min_peak:.2f}°C (req: {required_min_peak:.2f}°C) - PASSING\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nFailing: {len(results['failing_runs'])} out of {len(all_runs)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q04_conveyor_speed(thermocouples, mes_log):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 4: Check whether conveyor speed in each run is feasible.\n\n    From handbook:\n    - LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n    - PROCESS SPEED must be >= LINE SPEED (minimum)\n\n    The actual conveyor speed is given in the MES log.\n    We need to check if it meets the minimum required speed.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 4: Conveyor Speed Feasibility\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    all_runs = sorted(mes_log['run_id'].unique())\n\n    for run_id in all_runs:\n        mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n\n        # Get parameters\n        boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n        board_length_cm = mes_row['board_length_cm']\n        loading_factor = mes_row['loading_factor']\n        actual_speed = mes_row['conveyor_speed_cm_min']\n\n        # Calculate minimum required speed\n        # LINE SPEED (MINIMUM) = BOARDS_PER_MINUTE × LENGTH_PER_BOARD / LOAD_FACTOR\n        required_min_speed = (boards_per_min * board_length_cm) / loading_factor\n\n        # Check if actual speed meets requirement\n        meets = actual_speed >= required_min_speed\n\n        result = {\n            \"run_id\": run_id,\n            \"required_min_speed_cm_min\": round(required_min_speed, 2),\n            \"actual_speed_cm_min\": round(actual_speed, 2),\n            \"meets\": meets\n        }\n\n        results.append(result)\n\n        meets_str = \"MEETS\" if meets else \"FAILS\"\n        print(f\"  {run_id}: Actual = {actual_speed:.2f} cm/min, Required >= {required_min_speed:.2f} cm/min - {meets_str}\")\n\n    meets_count = sum(1 for r in results if r['meets'])\n    print(f\"\\nMeets requirement: {meets_count} out of {len(results)} runs\")\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef calculate_q05_best_run_per_family(thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects, q01_results, q02_results, q03_results, q04_results):\n    \"\"\"\n    Question 5: Select best run per board_family based on quality and efficiency.\n\n    Quality metrics:\n    - Profile compliance (preheat ramp < 2°C/s)\n    - TAL in range (30-60s)\n    - Peak temp meets requirement\n    - Defect yield (from test_defects)\n\n    Efficiency metrics:\n    - Conveyor speed feasibility\n    - Throughput (boards_per_min)\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*80)\n    print(\"QUESTION 5: Best Run Selection Per Board Family\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    results = []\n\n    # Get all board families\n    board_families = sorted(mes_log['board_family'].unique())\n\n    for board_family in board_families:\n        print(f\"\\n  Board Family: {board_family}\")\n\n        # Get all runs for this family\n        family_runs = mes_log[mes_log['board_family'] == board_family]['run_id'].tolist()\n\n        if len(family_runs) == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Score each run\n        run_scores = {}\n\n        for run_id in family_runs:\n            score = 0\n            violations = []\n\n            # Quality metrics\n\n            # 1. Preheat ramp compliance\n            if run_id not in q01_results['violating_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"preheat_ramp\")\n\n            # 2. TAL compliance\n            tal_result = next((r for r in q02_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if tal_result and tal_result['status'] == 'compliant':\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"tal\")\n\n            # 3. Peak temp compliance\n            if run_id not in q03_results['failing_runs']:\n                score += 10\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"peak_temp\")\n\n            # 4. Conveyor speed feasibility\n            speed_result = next((r for r in q04_results if r['run_id'] == run_id), None)\n            if speed_result and speed_result['meets']:\n                score += 5\n            else:\n                violations.append(\"conveyor_speed\")\n\n            # 5. Defect yield (from test_defects)\n            defect_summary = test_defects[(test_defects['run_id'] == run_id) &\n                                          (test_defects['inspection_stage'] == 'SUMMARY')]\n            if len(defect_summary) > 0:\n                yield_pct = defect_summary['fp_yield_pct'].values[0]\n                # Higher yield is better\n                score += yield_pct / 10  # Scale to 0-10 range\n\n            # Efficiency metrics\n\n            # 6. Throughput (boards per minute)\n            mes_row = mes_log[mes_log['run_id'] == run_id].iloc[0]\n            boards_per_min = mes_row['boards_per_min']\n            score += boards_per_min  # Higher throughput is better\n\n            # 7. Downtime penalty\n            downtime_s = mes_row['downtime_s']\n            score -= downtime_s / 10  # Penalize downtime\n\n            run_scores[run_id] = score\n\n            print(f\"    {run_id}: Score = {score:.2f}, Violations = {violations}\")\n\n        # Select best run (highest score)\n        if len(run_scores) > 0:\n            sorted_runs = sorted(run_scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n            best_run_id = sorted_runs[0][0]\n            runner_up_ids = [run_id for run_id, _ in sorted_runs[1:]]\n\n            result = {\n                \"board_family\": board_family,\n                \"best_run_id\": best_run_id,\n                \"runner_up_run_ids\": runner_up_ids\n            }\n\n            results.append(result)\n\n            print(f\"    Best run: {best_run_id} (Score: {run_scores[best_run_id]:.2f})\")\n\n    # Sort by board_family\n    results.sort(key=lambda x: x['board_family'])\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef convert_to_json_serializable(obj):\n    \"\"\"Convert numpy/pandas types to native Python types for JSON serialization.\"\"\"\n    if isinstance(obj, dict):\n        return {key: convert_to_json_serializable(value) for key, value in obj.items()}\n    elif isinstance(obj, list):\n        return [convert_to_json_serializable(item) for item in obj]\n    elif isinstance(obj, (np.integer, np.int64)):\n        return int(obj)\n    elif isinstance(obj, (np.floating, np.float64)):\n        return float(obj)\n    elif isinstance(obj, np.ndarray):\n        return obj.tolist()\n    elif pd.isna(obj):\n        return None\n    else:\n        return obj\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n    print(\"REFLOW MACHINE MAINTENANCE DATA ANALYSIS\")\n    print(\"=\"*80)\n\n    # Create output directory\n    output_dir = Path('/app/output')\n    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Load data\n    thermocouples, mes_log, test_defects = load_data()\n\n    print(f\"\\nData loaded:\")\n    print(f\"  Thermocouple readings: {len(thermocouples)} rows\")\n    print(f\"  MES log entries: {len(mes_log)} runs\")\n    print(f\"  Test defect records: {len(test_defects)} rows\")\n\n    #
